- **m-z-rt-feature-matching**: Use when you have extracted peaks from multiple LC/HRMS batches (n &gt; 1) with their m/z and RT values, and you need to identify and align peaks representing the same compound across batches to build a consensus feature matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/m-z-rt-feature-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **m-z-to-normalized-kendrick-mass-conversion**: Use when you have uploaded peak list data containing m/z values and wish to construct a Kendrick mass plot where alkane homolog series (or other homologous families) are expected to appear as horizontal lines. - **m-z-tolerance-window-matching**: Use when when you have detected features with m/z, drift time, and retention time dimensions and need to associate peaks into isotopic groups (e.g., monoisotopes with C13 substitutions) or align features across multiple LC-IMS-MS/MS samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/m-z-tolerance-window-matching) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **m-z-value-clustering-mass-spectrometry**: Use when processing extracted peak lists from MSI data and you need to annotate matrix-related signals but suspect that multiple ions with the same or very similar m/z values (isobaric ions) are present.
